The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [265] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级模块化框架，用于遥操作机械臂的主动碰撞规避，直接作用于末端执行器速度指令，通过TTC预测和罗德里格斯旋转实现避障，并避免死锁问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类操作员在遥操作中可能因网络延迟或注意力不足而无法快速避开突然出现的障碍物，造成安全风险。

Method: 框架预处理点云后，基于碰撞时间（TTC）预测潜在碰撞，并利用罗德里格斯旋转公式改变相对速度方向但保持大小，以规避障碍并克服传统人工势场法的死锁问题。

Result: 仿真表明该方法在多种场景下末端执行器避障率高于基线方法，物理机器人实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效提升遥操作中的碰撞规避能力，且轻量级设计满足高频控制需求。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [266] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出嵌套策略学习框架NestDex，通过学习的手部技能辅助演示收集，降低灵巧操作演示采集难度，提升策略学习效率与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作演示收集困难，因需协调手臂运动与精细手指行为，传统直接指定手指轨迹方法不可靠且效率低。

Method: 嵌套策略框架，操作员控制手臂并通过单自由度离合器调节主动手部技能，内层策略基于本体感觉历史适应，视觉语言选择器激活合适技能，外层策略部署时独立运行，使用手部动作变分自编码器压缩手部动作目标。

Result: 真实世界实验表明NestDex提高演示可靠性和效率，支持有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex有效缓解灵巧操作演示收集瓶颈，提升策略学习效能。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [267] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FIRE-VLA框架，利用失败信息通过自蒸馏改进自动驾驶VLA模型，在保持单样本规划性能的同时显著降低轨迹误差和失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO方法在组内所有轨迹都较差时，只能排序失败而无法引导策略跳出失败区域，导致失败持续存在。

Method: FIRE-VLA在低奖励、低多样性组中触发自蒸馏，使用冻结的轮次起始模型作为教师，仅教师可见未来轨迹，监督学生生成前缀的答案token，同时保留GRPO。

Result: 在150个保留场景的6019个样本上，G=4平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500米，评估持久失败率从13.03%降至11.20%，单样本规划相当。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA通过将未解决的失败转化为特权监督，有效改善极端轨迹，降低失败率，且无需外部大模型。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>


### [268] [Capstan-driven Continuum Surgical Robot: Design, Modeling, and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13396)
*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于驱动-感知协同设计的集成框架，通过在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件测量缆绳张力，结合多体短粗梁模型的并行计算和近端力/力矩传感器，实现连续体手术机器人的实时形状与力感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于绞盘组件空间受限，难以获取缆绳张力信息，导致形状和力感知成为连续体手术机器人发展的瓶颈。

Method: 采用驱动-感知协同设计，在电机支架中增加柔性元件以测量缆绳张力；提出基于多体短粗梁模型的并行计算框架，处理空间缆绳布线带来的复杂关节构型，并利用近端力/力矩传感器作为附加测量锚点，实现稳定感知。

Result: 在单段和双段连续体机器人上验证了框架，实现了实时尖端位姿估计以及接触力和接触位置感知。

Conclusion: 该框架在不占用紧凑绞盘空间的前提下提供缆绳张力反馈，使连续体手术机器人的集成感知成为可能。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [269] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动技能学习算法 Deliberate Practice (DP)，在有限练习预算下，通过双线性规划计算预算最优的技能分配，最大化期望累计奖励，并在模拟和真实长时程操作任务中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能学习方法未考虑练习预算有限的问题，导致机器人可能浪费练习时间在低效或无法在预算内掌握的技能上，影响长时程任务的最终表现。

Method: 将技能学习建模为有限预算下的分配问题，通过双线性规划精确求解预算最优的练习计划，同时估计技能掌握所需时间和技能解锁任务的累计奖励。

Result: 在模拟和真实的长时程操作任务中，DP 方法相比基线能在有限练习时间内获得更高累计奖励，提高长时程规划成功率。

Conclusion: DP 提供了一种理论上有保证且实际可行的方式，让机器人在有限练习预算下高效学习并提升复杂任务表现，展示了双线性规划在技能学习中的潜力。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget. DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [270] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nick Heppert,Johannes Fabian Hahn,Abhinav Valada,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Seeker，一种任务和状态条件化的注意力读出机制，通过动作监督学习视觉区域兴趣（ROI），无需外部标签，改善机器人视觉运动学习的数据效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉瓶颈方法依赖外部空间标签（如注视、对象类别）或动作衍生裁剪，后者在视觉证据远离末端执行器或随任务进展变化时可能失准，且固定裁剪尺度和事件时序限制性能。

Method: Seeker从冻结的DINOv3特征出发，通过迭代更新查询与视觉证据，从动作监督中学习进度感知的ROI，用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强和点云过滤。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上，Seeker优于无裁剪、增强和动作衍生裁剪基线；真实场景中，域内成功率从最佳基线的48.3%提升至76.7%，光照/背景变化下从20.0%提升至60.0%。

Conclusion: Seeker通过动作监督学习自适应ROI，有效解耦“看哪里”和“如何做”，显著提升数据效率和鲁棒性，尤其适用于视觉证据随任务变化的情况。

Abstract: Visual bottlenecks that focus policy inputs on regions of interest (ROIs) can improve data-efficient visuomotor learning by separating where to look from how to act. Many ROI interfaces rely on external spatial labels, such as gaze, object classes, or affordance annotations. Label-free alternatives often derive crops from trajectories by detecting gripper or motion events and centering a fixed crop at the projected end-effector. Such action-derived crops are useful spatial priors that require no additional labels, but they encode fixed choices about event timing, proxy points, and crop scale. When the visual evidence needed for control lies away from the end-effector or changes continuously with task progress, these crops can become misaligned. We propose Seeker, a task- and state-conditioned readout that learns attention from action. Starting from frozen DINOv3 features, Seeker iteratively updates a query with gathered visual evidence, producing progression-aware ROIs solely from action supervision. The learned ROI serves as a spatial interface for RGB cropping, mask-guided background augmentation, and point-cloud filtering. In simulation and the real world, Seeker improves data efficiency and robustness over no-crop, augmentation, and action-derived crop baselines. On real robots, Seeker raises average in-domain success from the best baseline's 48.3% to 76.7% and success under lighting/background shifts from 20.0% to 60.0%.

</details>


### [271] [ContactGuard: Pre-Contact Execution Monitoring with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13438)
*Gehan Zheng,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContactGuard是一种预接触执行监视器，通过潜在视觉空间预测动作块后果，在接触前提前中止失败操作，显著提升接触丰富操纵任务的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在接触后才检测失败，而腕部相机设置下不良接近可能已造成物体位移等损害，需要预接触阶段的实时故障预警。

Method: 利用无标签轨迹训练潜在世界模型，预测多视角视觉嵌入；用少量标注预接触片段训练轻量级失败探测器，在接触前锚定预测并验证后接触潜在状态。

Result: 在真实世界接触丰富任务中，比直接和扰动消融更准确预测失败，并成功迁移至实时机器人预接触中止信号。

Conclusion: ContactGuard能有效提前识别失败风险，无需修改底层策略即可提升任务稳定性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation failures are often detected only after the robot has committed to contact. This is especially limiting in wrist-camera setups: close gripper--object views help observe contact, but a poor approach may already push, miss, slip, or disturb the object before conventional detectors react. We introduce \emph{ContactGuard}, a pre-contact execution monitor for chunked visuomotor policies. Given the policy's planned action chunk, ContactGuard predicts its short-horizon consequence in latent visual space and aborts if the predicted future latent indicates likely failure. Its latent world model is trained from unlabelled robot trajectories to predict compact multi-view visual embeddings under planned actions, avoiding pixel-level video prediction. A lightweight failure probe is then trained from a small labelled set of pre-contact clips. At deployment, ContactGuard anchors prediction before an imminent contact event, rolls the model forward under the policy's own actions, and verifies the predicted post-contact latent. Across real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ContactGuard predicts failure more accurately than direct and corrupted-action ablations, and transfers to live robot as a pre-contact abort signal without modifying the underlying policy.

</details>


### [272] [Mind the Context: Continual Learning of Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via Environmental-Social Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13448)
*Rafal Robert Karpinski,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Nikhil Churamani,Yiming Luo,Maartje M. A. de Graaf,Davide Dell'Anna,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EDD框架，通过显式分离环境与社会知识并采用重放机制，解决社交机器人在域增量场景下的持续学习问题，实验表明其优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需在多样环境中适应不同社会规范，但现有持续学习研究未覆盖域增量场景，即环境与社会线索共同影响行动适宜性的情况。

Method: 提出Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD)框架，显式分离环境与社会代理相关知识，并使用基于重放的排练机制来缓解遗忘。

Result: EDD在多个室内域的任务中优于多种最先进基线，消融实验验证了不同解缠策略和域顺序的敏感性。

Conclusion: EDD有效提升机器人在未知域增量环境中的持续学习能力，为社交机器人的适应性行为生成提供了新方法。

Abstract: Social robots are expected to operate across diverse environments, where similar arrangements can imply different socially appropriate actions, e.g., starting a conversation may be acceptable in a crowded home but disruptive in an office meeting. Because such norms and environments cannot all be anticipated in advance, robots require continual learning (CL) to adapt from sequential experience while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Prior work has studied CL for generating socially appropriate robot actions, but it has not addressed domain-incremental settings in which the robot incrementally encounters diverse contexts (e.g., living room, meeting room, office, hallway), where both environmental (e.g., whether the space is open or cluttered with furniture) and social cues (e.g., how people or other agents are positioned around the robot) jointly shape the appropriateness of robot actions. We address this gap with the Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD) framework. EDD explicitly separates environmental and social-agent related knowledge and uses replay-based rehearsal to mitigate forgetting while learning the appropriateness of robot actions (e.g., cleaning, serving, starting a conversation) across several indoor domains. Experiments show that EDD outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines, and ablation studies further evaluate different disentanglement strategies and the sensitivity to domain ordering. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git.

</details>


### [273] [Decoding Task Progress from VLA Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13474)
*Atiksh Bhardwaj,Edward Weiyi Duan,Prithwish Dan,Wei-Chiu Ma,Preston Culbertson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉-语言-动作模型π0.5的残差流中线性编码了任务进度信号，该信号在预训练骨干中已存在，可用于无标签的分布外检测和策略监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型正快速部署为通用操作策略，但缺乏理解其内部表示和运行时监控的工具。

Method: 利用机械可解释性方法，在π0.5的残差流上训练线性探针来读取任务进度；并基于探针构建无标签的OOD检测器。

Result: 任务进度信号可从激活中线性读取，预训练骨干中已存在；探针可泛化到未见任务，且对语言反事实敏感，但无法有效引导策略；作为OOD检测器与SOTA方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: VLA模型内部存在丰富的线性可读语义表示（如任务进度），学习读取这些信号为监控部署中的视觉运动策略提供了轻量级、可解释的路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are moving rapidly towards deployment as general-purpose manipulation policies, but we currently lack basic tools for understanding what these models represent internally or for monitoring them at runtime. Leveraging ideas from mechanistic interpretability, we probe the residual stream of $π_{0.5}$ and find that task progress, the normalized time remaining in a trajectory, is linearly readable from the activations. We find that this signal is present in the pretrained PaliGemma backbone prior to training on any robot-specific data. A single linear probe generalizes to unseen tasks and varies under language counterfactuals when trained on multi-prompt data, but does not enable meaningful steering of the policy. These properties make the signal directly useful for instrumenting deployed VLAs. We use the probe as a simple label-free OOD detector, which detects stalled task progress, and find it competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our results suggest that VLAs have rich, linearly readable internal representations of semantic quantities like task progress, and that learning to read these signals offers a lightweight, interpretable path toward monitoring deployed visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [274] [A Browser-Native Digital Test Range for Benchmarking 4D Ocean-Glider Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13511)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于浏览器的数字测试场，用于在可复现的4D环境中评估海洋滑翔机规划器，并通过OSSE和实地审计验证了其有效性，同时实现了GliderFlight 1.2.0的浏览器内高保真模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋滑翔机规划器的重复实地评估受限于稀缺的车辆、操作员、部署回收资源，且海洋条件无法重置，因此需要一种可重复、低成本、可比较的测试环境。

Method: 构建了浏览器原生的数字测试场，提供从区域选择到评分的一体化流程，定义了统一的计划到观测契约，支持手动、内置及导入的规划算法，并通过Pyodide/WebAssembly实现GliderFlight 1.2.0的本地到浏览器保真迁移。

Result: 在OSSE中，5种经典规划器在54次任务中均成功完成且无严重违规，揭示了规划器排名和潜水策略的权衡；公开部署提供了现场审计，验证了运动学边界；GliderFlight 1.2.0在浏览器中实现了与本地一致的结果。

Conclusion: 该测试场提供了科学可追溯且组件合格的运行空间，适用于任务规模部署前的实验，为海洋滑翔机规划器的可重复评估建立了新途径。

Abstract: Repeated in-situ evaluation of ocean-glider planners requires scarce vehicles, operators, deployment and recovery resources, and ocean conditions that cannot be reset for competing algorithms. We present a guided, installation-free browser-native digital test range that transforms a selected region into a reproducible four-dimensional experiment. The system leads users from regional domain selection through mission-scoped bathymetry, time/depth forcing, science objectives, optional task decomposition, route specification, current-advected execution, observation generation, and scoring. Its primary contribution is a common plan-to-observation contract unifying vehicle, sensing, and evaluator assumptions across manual routes, transparent built-in algorithms, and imported classical or learned-planner outputs, while exported artifacts form dataset-ready records. A controlled Observing System Simulation Experiment (OSSE) evaluates five classical planners in two episodes, three deterministic seeds, and a calibrated 60-hour horizon. All 54 missions completed and recovered without hard violations, while planner rankings and dive-policy effects revealed operational-scientific tradeoffs. An authentic public deployment supplied a field-referenced audit to scope current kinematic boundaries. Separately, source-locked GliderFlight 1.2.0 achieved native-to-browser parity through Pyodide/WebAssembly, establishing a pathway for high-fidelity multi-tier simulation. The resulting operational space is scientifically traceable and component-qualified for mission-scale pre-deployment experimentation.

</details>


### [275] [HumanTracker: Towards Comprehensive and Human-Aligned Motion Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13555)
*Dairu Liu,Zekun Qi,Jiayu Zeng,Ruixi Yu,Yu Guan,Yintianrun Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Sikai Liang,Zekai Li,Chenghuai Lin,Xinqiang Yu,Wenyao Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了HumanTracker基准和HumanScore指标，用于更符合人类感知的人形机器人运动跟踪评估，该基准包含153小时的光学运动轨迹，HumanScore基于12K运动对训练，能更好地预测人类偏好并识别接触和稳定性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形运动跟踪评估指标（如运动学误差）与人类感知不一致，忽略了对人类感知重要的物理伪影（如脚滑动、触地时间错误）；同时现有测试集规模小且缺乏多样性，无法充分评估接触丰富和长时间的行为。

Method: 构建HumanTracker基准，包含约153小时多专业表演者的光学运动轨迹，分为四个运动族并带有文本标签；提出HumanScore，一种基于偏好对齐的度量，在12K运动对（含24K运动）上训练。

Result: 在多个代表性的最先进跟踪器上，HumanScore比运动学指标更好地预测人类偏好，并揭示了运动学指标常常忽略的接触和稳定性失败。

Conclusion: HumanTracker和HumanScore为人类运动跟踪评估提供了与人类感知一致的、可扩展的解决方案，有助于更准确地评估和诊断跟踪器性能。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is central to teleoperation and whole-body imitation, yet evaluation often disagrees with what people perceive in videos. Kinematic errors average per-frame pose differences but miss the physical artifacts that matter most, particularly unstable support and incorrect contacts such as foot skating and mistimed touch-downs. Meanwhile, widely used test suites are small and lack the diversity needed to stress contact-rich, long-horizon behaviors. We introduce HumanTracker to make humanoid tracking evaluation both perceptually aligned and scalable. The HumanTracker benchmark contains approximately 153 hours of optical motion trajectories from multiple professional performers, organized into four motion families with text labels for fine-grained diagnosis. We further propose HumanScore, a preference-aligned metric trained on 12K motion pairs containing 24K motions. Across representative state-of-the-art trackers, HumanScore better predicts human preferences and reveals contact and stability failures that kinematic metrics often miss.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [276] [Excitation-Supervised Closed-Loop Self-Calibration and Target Seeking for an Unknown-Pose Range-Bearing Relay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12528)
*Yash Bagla*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种闭环激励监督控制器，利用轨迹扩展裕度在线判断中继标定是否可信，并在不可信时自动触发探索运动，以保证目标跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅静态分类观测窗口，无法在线决定何时标定可信及不可信时如何行动，导致固定标定调度在激励不足时性能下降。

Method: 利用轨迹扩展裕度$S_v$作为可辨识性指标，同时充当噪声种子精度界、局部向量方差分解和圆几何激励预算，设计激励监督控制器：当证书不足时触发探索，并将目标跟踪输入投影远离激励方向；证书充分后切换到无限制目标跟踪。

Result: 仿真和实验表明，监督方法在固定调度激励不足时保持高精度（偏航RMSE 0.0095-0.0191 rad，100%成功），而固定基线性能下降（偏航RMSE升至0.065 rad，成功率降至56%）。

Conclusion: 所提监督方法能自适应获取所需激励，保证标定可信后实现目标跟踪收敛，阈值可由期望标定精度确定，优于固定调度。

Abstract: A vehicle seeking a hidden target through a range-bearing relay of unknown position and yaw must decide, online, whether its own motion has already made the relay calibration trustworthy, and what to do when it has not. Two distinct vehicle-relative observations are known to remove the calibration gauge and make the target's relay-local packet globally actionable (arXiv:2608.09464), but that statement is static: it classifies a stored window only after the fact. This paper supplies the closed-loop layer: we show that the trajectory-spread margin $S_v$ that governs identifiability is simultaneously a finite-noise seed-accuracy bound, a local-vector variance decomposition, and a circle-geometry excitation budget, and we use it to supervise an excitation-reset controller. An excitation-supervised algorithm retriggers exploratory motion whenever the spread certificate is insufficient, projecting the target-seeking input away from the excitation's push, and otherwise proceeds to unrestricted target seeking. Under explicit sampling assumptions the supervision rule provably acquires any required excitation in finite time; in the noiseless local regime with positive excitation decay, estimator convergence yields target-seeking convergence after certification; and the threshold is selected from a desired calibration-accuracy level rather than chosen heuristically. Closed-loop simulation, paired Monte Carlo comparisons, a spread-threshold ablation, and a ROS 2/Gazebo software-in-the-loop experiment with sensing delay validate the approach. A decay-rate sweep shows that supervision matters when a fixed schedule's decay outruns the unknown time-to-adequate-excitation: over 100 paired trials the fixed baseline's yaw RMSE rises from 0.010 to 0.065 rad and success falls to 56%, while target-tracking error remains insensitive; supervision keeps yaw RMSE between 0.0095 and 0.0191 rad with 100% success.

</details>


### [277] [The $θ$-Symmetric SRG with Applications to Stability of Cactus Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12591)
*Xiaokan Yang,Wei Chen,Li Qiu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了θ-对称缩放相对图（SRG）并将其应用于仙人掌网络的稳定性分析，通过建立与范数最小化问题的联系实现相位计算，并利用代数性质推导出网络稳定性的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SRG定义无法刻画相位超前/滞后行为，且缺乏多变量扩展的自然性，需要更直观且保守性更低的稳定性分析工具。

Method: 提出θ-对称SRG，分别分析增益与相位特性，建立相位与范数最小化的联系，通过半定规划计算相位，并推导子乘性和子加性性质，应用于循环和并行反馈网络的鲁棒稳定性分析。

Result: 建立了θ-对称SRG的代数性质，为循环和并行反馈网络提供了稳定性的充要条件，统一了多环仙人掌网络的稳定性框架，并减少了保守性，提供了几何解释。

Conclusion: θ-对称SRG框架有效且更直观，通过示例验证了其在仙人掌网络稳定性分析中的实用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we systematically study a variant of the scaled relative graph (SRG), referred to as the $θ$-symmetric SRG, and apply it to the stability analysis of cactus networks. Compared with the previous SRG definition, the $θ$-symmetric SRG enables the characterization of phase lead and lag behaviors, and serves as a more natural multivariable extension of the classical Nyquist plot. We first analyze the gain and phase aspects of $θ$-symmetric SRG separately and build a connection between $θ$-segmental phase and a norm minimization problem. This connection makes it possible to compute $θ$-segmental phase via semidefinite programming. We further derive the submultiplicative and subadditive properties of $θ$-symmetric SRG. These algebraic properties are crucial to determine the nonsingularity of product-type and sum-type return difference matrices, which topologically correspond to the cyclic and parallel-feedback extreme cases of cactus networks. By taking the cyclic interconnection as the fundamental starting point, we establish necessary and sufficient conditions for its robust stability. Integrating this with the parallel case, we synthesize a unified stability framework for general multi-loop cactus networks. The $θ$-symmetric SRG framework is less conservative and provides a more intuitive geometric interpretation of system behaviors compared with some existing approaches. Several examples are included to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [278] [Security-Constrained Operation of IBR-Dominated Power Systems: Static and Dynamic Security Across Preventive and Corrective Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12609)
*Buxin She*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两轴视角（静态与动态安全、预防与纠正决策时机）来审视逆变器资源（IBRs）下的安全约束运行，并综述了现有公式、方法和能力表示，指出IBR能力可扩大可行域或缓解安全约束，但共享能力与约束决定其可交付性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆变器资源（IBRs）将电力系统运行与快速动态和控制器依赖响应耦合，其可配置能力正在重塑安全约束运行的制定与协调，需要系统化理解这些变化。

Method: 提出一个通用公式来显式表达预防-纠正权衡，并在此框架下综述和综合现有公式、方法和能力表示。

Result: 两个发现：1）IBR能力可扩大可行集或缓解安全约束，降低运行成本或提高安全性能；2）共享能力和约束决定这些能力在运行中是否可交付。

Conclusion: 这些发现激励了未来在IBR能力表征与量化、联合调度和可扩展求解框架方面的研究。

Abstract: Inverter-based resources (IBRs) couple power system operation to fast dynamics and controller-dependent responses. Their configurable capabilities are reshaping the formulation and coordination of security-constrained operation. This paper presents a two-axis view: static versus dynamic security and preventive versus corrective decision timing. Preventive scheduling is extending from static post-contingency feasibility toward dynamic security, while corrective operation spans equilibrium-based corrective actions and trajectory-based control. A generic formulation represents this change and makes the preventive--corrective tradeoff explicit. Existing formulations, methods, and capability representations are reviewed and synthesized within this framework. The surveyed work yields two findings. First, IBR capability can expand the feasible set or relieve security constraints, reducing operating cost or improving security performance. Second, shared capability and constraints across formulations determine whether that capability remains operationally deliverable. These findings motivate future research in IBR capability characterization and quantification, joint scheduling, and scalable solution frameworks.

</details>


### [279] [Time Distribution Principle Using Measured Traveling Waves in Power Grid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12730)
*Haozong Wang,Yayu Yang,Jiahui Yang,Muhamman Umar Afzaal,Yuru Wu,Yan Wen,Zhengfa Zhang,Yilu Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于行波测量的电力系统时间同步新原理，利用正反向行波传播的对称性实现高精度时间同步，无需外部时间参考，仿真验证达到微秒级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间同步方法（如卫星同步和基于通信的方法）面临信号脆弱性和依赖通信延迟对称性的问题，需要新的替代方案。

Method: 提出基于行波测量的时间同步原理，利用电力系统中正反向行波传播的固有对称性进行时间分布，并通过电磁暂态仿真（修改的IEEE 14节点系统）验证。

Result: 在正常条件下，该方法实现了微秒级的时间同步精度。

Conclusion: 基于行波的时间同步原理可作为传统方法的潜在替代方案，提高安全性并减少对通信质量的依赖。

Abstract: Accurate time synchronization is essential for distributed systems. Conventional methods, such as satellite-based synchronization and communication-based approaches, face challenges including signal vulnerability and dependence on communication delay symmetry. This paper proposes a novel time synchronization principle based on traveling wave (TW) measurements in power grids. By leveraging the inherent symmetry of forward and backward TW propagation, the proposed method achieves high-precision time distribution without relying on external time references. The methodology is validated through electromagnetic transient simulations on a modified IEEE 14-bus system. The results demonstrate that under normal conditions, the proposed approach achieves microsecond-level synchronization accuracy. These findings suggest that the TW-based time synchronization principle is a potential alternative to traditional methods, offering improved security and reduced dependence on communication quality.

</details>


### [280] [Improving Fast Charging Safety With Core Temperature Estimation Via Kolmogorov-Arnold Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12638)
*Faysal Ahamed,Tanushree Roy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Kolmogorov-Arnold网络与鲁棒控制屏障函数的锂电池快速充电框架，在无法直接测量核心温度的情况下，通过估计温度并施加安全约束，实现安全且快速的充电。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂电池快速充电会导致温度显著上升，带来安全风险。由于电池核心温度无法直接测量，实时安全保障面临挑战，因此需要一种能够利用可测数据估计核心温度并施加安全约束的充电策略。

Method: 采用Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）估计电池核心温度，并将其嵌入鲁棒控制屏障函数（rCBF）约束中。利用表面温度、冷却液温度、冷却功率和充电电流等可测数据，求解二次规划问题以得到最优充电电流，同时考虑估计误差和模型不确定性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法能在保持电池温度安全的同时，实现与现有先进方法相当的充电时间，而现有方法无法保证同等水平的热安全。

Conclusion: KAN-rCBF框架能够有效处理核心温度不可测的问题，在保证安全的前提下实现快速充电，为电池热管理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Fast charging of Lithium-ion batteries can lead to a significant temperature rise, which can cause serious risks to battery safety and lifetime. To ensure safe battery operation, thermal constraints must be enforced during the fast charging process. However, the core temperature of the battery cannot be directly measured in practice, which makes real-time safety enforcement challenging. This paper proposes a framework that incorporates core temperature estimates from Kolmogorov-Arnold Network within robust control barrier function (KAN-rCBF) constraints for battery fast-charging. The algorithm utilizes measurements from battery surface temperature, coolant temperature, coolant power, and charging current to solve a quadratic programming problem under safety constraints. We prescribe analytical safety guarantees for this optimal charging policy under KAN estimation errors and model uncertainty. Simulation results show that the proposed method maintains a safe battery temperature while achieving charging times comparable to the state-of-the-art method, where the latter fails to guarantee the same level of thermal safety.

</details>


### [281] [Technical Report on Resilient and Secure Large-Scale Energy Internet Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12916)
*Ioannis Zografopoulos,Karen Largman,Isaac Ortega Romero,S M Zia Ur Rashid,Yexiang Chen,George Fragkos,Charalambos Konstantinou,Subhash Lakshminarayana,Juan Ospina,Airin Rahman,Suman Rath,Vivek Kumar Singh,Mucun Sun,Wei Sun*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本报告全面审视了大规模能源互联网的安全与韧性挑战，涵盖威胁态势、检测与缓解技术、建模与决策框架、人工智能可信部署及图路由方案，并提出了研究、标准化和监管建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源互联网将电力、信息和市场层深度耦合，数字化进程带来了新的安全与韧性风险，亟需系统性评估和应对方案。

Method: 通过Task Force专家组调研，综述威胁态势和现有技术，提出涵盖检测、建模、控制、决策及AI可信部署的框架，并引入图论抗毁路由方法。

Result: 形成了威胁分类、检测与缓解技术综述、互依赖建模与决策框架、AI风险与可信方案、图路由设计，以及研究/标准/监管建议。

Conclusion: 大规模能源互联网的安全韧性需多维度技术与政策协同，重点加强跨域建模、AI可信性和信息路由抗毁能力，并需标准化和监管支持。

Abstract: This IEEE PES Task Force report examines the security and resilience of large-scale Energy Internet (EI) systems, in which electricity, information, and market layers are tightly coupled through pervasive digitalization. The report characterizes the EI cyber-physical threat landscape and surveys detection, assurance, and mitigation techniques, presents modeling, control, and decision-making frameworks that capture cyber-physical interdependencies, including storage integration, multi-dimensional resilience, and electricity price forecasting, examines adversarial risks and trustworthy deployment of artificial intelligence, and introduces graph-based, attack-resilient information routing. The report closes with recommendations for research, standardization, and regulatory efforts needed to realize a resilient and secure large-scale EI.

</details>


### [282] [AoI-Guaranteed Dynamic Route Planning for Connected Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13083)
*Sajedeh Norouzi,Maryam Ansarifard,Farshad Zeinali,Ali Nouruzi,Nader Mokari,Hamid Saeedi,Nizar Zorba*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合旅行时间估计与无线资源可用性的双因素动态路径规划方案AGDRP，利用深度强化学习在保障信息时效性的同时优化路径选择，仿真表明其优于仅考虑旅行时间的基线方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态路径规划仅关注路线容量和旅行时间，未能考虑车联网中通信资源的时效性（如信息年龄），导致数据传输延迟或过时，影响系统性能。

Method: 采用深度强化学习（DRL）构建双目标优化模型，同时最小化旅行时间和信息年龄（AoI），通过自适应学习实现动态路径规划（AGDRP）。

Result: 仿真结果显示AGDRP在平衡旅行时间与AoI方面优于传统仅优化旅行时间的方案，且加入AoI最小化显著提升了路径规划性能。

Conclusion: 在车联网路径规划中纳入通信资源时效性（AoI）能有效提升整体性能，AGDRP为智能交通系统提供了更优的路径决策方案。

Abstract: The advancement of Intelligent Transportation Sys- tems (ITS) has been significantly driven by progress in radio communication technology. Dynamic route planning, a key com- ponent of ITS, traditionally focuses on metrics such as route capacity and travel time. This paper presents a novel dual- factor approach that integrates travel time estimation and radio resource availability into an innovative route-planning scheme for connected vehicles (CVs). To address this dual-objective route planning challenge, we employ Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL). Our approach, called AoI-Guaranteed Dynamic Route Planning (AGDRP), effectively balances travel time and Age of Information (AoI), enhancing route planning performance through adaptive learning over time. Simulation results demon- strate that AGDRP outperforms the baseline scheme, which solely focuses on travel time optimization. In fact, we show that incor- porating AoI minimization significantly enhances route planning performance beyond conventional travel-time-based approaches.

</details>


### [283] [Robust Space-Filling Input Design via Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13360)
*Máté Kiss,Roland Tóth,Maarten Schoukens*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种鲁棒的填充空间输入设计算法，该算法对模型类整体进行优化，以应对假设模型与真实系统不匹配的问题，并通过模拟示例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的空间填充输入设计依赖于假设模型，当真实系统与假设差异较大时，设计信号性能会显著下降。因此需要一种对模型类鲁棒的设计方法。

Method: 该算法对模型类上最优性准则的期望进行优化，并采用随机逼近技术来求解该鲁棒准则的优化问题。

Result: 仿真示例表明所提出的算法能够有效生成针对整个模型类的空间填充数据集，并在模型失配情况下保持良好性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒空间填充输入设计算法为模型不确定性下的输入设计提供了有效解决方案，提高了设计信号对模型误差的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The space-filling input design approach generates a so-called space-filling dataset in the feature space of the system model. The design method is applicable on a broad class of model structures with wide selection of signals and also incorporates information measures through optimality criteria into the signal design. However, during the signal design, knowledge of a hypothesized model is required. The designed signal can perform far from the optimal if the true system is significantly different from the hypothesized system model. This paper proposes a robust space-filling input design algorithm that can generate a space-filling dataset for an entire class of models. The proposed algorithm takes the expectation of an optimality criterion over the population of the model class, and a stochastic approximation technique is employed to optimize this robust criteria. The efficiency of the proposed algorithm is demonstrated in a simulation example.

</details>


### [284] [Process-Aware Cross-Layer Adaptation for O-RAN-Enabled Industrial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13372)
*Elahe Delavari,Junaid Farooq,M. Majid Butt,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种过程感知的协同自适应引擎框架，通过联合优化无线网络、边缘计算和传感配置，以端到端任务成功率评估闭环工业应用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单独衡量吞吐量、延迟和推理精度无法反映闭环应用是否完成任务，需要一种新的过程感知评估和优化方法。

Method: 设计Process-Aware Co-adaptation Engine，整合应用结果、过程状态、无线电遥测、边缘计算状态和传感配置，选择跨完整回路的协调工作点。

Result: 在工厂检测数字孪生与5G O-RAN网络实验中，证明了资源分配随生产速度变化，独立调整各组件可能低效，且少量全系统评估即可找到高效配置。

Conclusion: 提出的协同自适应框架能有效优化闭环工业应用性能，优于独立调整方法，并具有实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Wireless networks increasingly support closed-loop industrial applications in which sensed data must be transmitted, processed, and converted into an action before physical process makes the result obsolete. Throughput, latency, and inference accuracy measured separately cannot determine whether such an application completed a useful task. We propose a Process-Aware Co-adaptation Engine framework that combines application outcomes, process state, radio telemetry, edge-compute state, and sensing configuration to select coordinated operating points across the complete loop. We evaluate the proposed approach in a factory-inspection case study that integrates a physics-based digital twin, a programmable 5G O-RAN network, and edge-based visual inference. The experiments show that the preferred resource allocation changes with production speed and that adapting individual system components independently can be inefficient. We further show that efficient configurations can be identified with relatively few full-system evaluations.

</details>


### [285] [AaLLM: An End-to-End Analog Circuit Design Framework from Topology Generation to Sizing Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13472)
*Mohammed Ayman Habib,Rylan Hart,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端的多代理LLM工作流AaLLM，自动从用户规格生成电路网表，涵盖拓扑生成和尺寸设计，通过RAG和三元反馈系统减少迭代，提升效率并创新拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模拟电路设计耗时且依赖专家经验，现有LLM方法仅处理部分任务，需手动添加技术知识，易产生幻觉，迭代繁琐，且无法创新拓扑导致次优设计。

Method: 构建多代理LLM工作流，自动从文献构建知识库，采用RAG模拟专家知识，并设计三元反馈系统（设计师、评论家、评估者）协调电路参数设定与迭代。

Result: 生成的拓扑在品质因数上与已知拓扑相当，某些电路提升3倍；SPICE调用次数减少3-4.5倍，墙钟时间减少40倍。

Conclusion: AaLLM实现了端到端自动化设计，减少人工干预，提高效率，并支持新型拓扑生成，为模拟电路设计提供创新方案。

Abstract: Analog circuit design is a time-consuming, iterative process in a nonlinear and high-dimensional design space that relies heavily on expert intuition. Among recent developments, LLMs have introduced a promising approach by bringing natural language reasoning to circuit design tasks. The majority of conventional LLM-based approaches provide fragmented solutions that focus either only on sizing or topology generation. These methods require adding specific technical knowledge manually, which is inefficient and prone to hallucinations during circuit sizing. Moreover, the inherent trade-off in meeting different specs makes current approaches iterative and tedious. Another shortcoming is the inability to create innovative topologies, which may lead to sub-optimal designs due to reliance on conventional topologies. In this paper, we present AaLLM, an open-source end-to-end multi-agent LLM workflow that takes user specs as input and outputs the appropriate netlist, encompassing both topology generation and circuit sizing. AaLLM automates the creation of a relevant knowledge base from research papers and textbooks to combat tedious manual data collection. A RAG model is implemented to emulate circuit design expertise using this knowledge base. Moreover, AaLLM uses a novel tri-agent feedback system comprising a Designer that determines circuit component values, a Critic that scrutinizes these values, and an Evaluator that minimizes circuit sizing iterations by arbitrating between the other two agents. AaLLM-generated novel topologies achieve a figure of merit (FoM) comparable to that of known topologies, and up to 3x higher for certain circuits. Testing on several circuit topologies, our results show a 3x - 4.5x decrease in the number of SPICE calls at inference when compared to SOTA multi-agent LLM pipelines. The results also show a 40x decrease in wall-clock time compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [286] [Safety-Critical Control for Quadrotor UAVs via Decentralized Navigation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13507)
*Omayra Yago Nieto,Alexandre Anahory Simoes,Leonardo Colombo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对四旋翼无人机编队在分布式导航函数驱动下的安全关键控制问题，提出了推力-姿态实现方法，并通过鲁棒HOCBF-QP安全过滤器确保高概率的成对防撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机只能沿机体垂直轴产生推力，而分布式导航函数产生的是全驱动平移参考力，直接应用存在动力学不匹配，可能导致不安全行为。

Method: 构造推力-姿态实现来近似全驱动参考动力学，并量化误差；在此基础上设计聚合鲁棒HOCBF-QP安全过滤器，对名义推力进行最小修正，以保证高概率的成对避碰。

Result: 该方法在存在学习模型不确定性的情况下，能够实现四旋翼编队的避碰控制，且对名义推力的修改最小。

Conclusion: 提出的控制框架有效解决了四旋翼无人机在模型不确定性下的安全控制问题，为实际应用提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We study safety-critical control for teams of quadrotor UAVs driven by decentralized navigation functions under learned model uncertainty. These functions generate fully actuated translational reference forces, while quadrotors can only produce thrust along their body-fixed vertical axes. We construct a thrust-attitude implementation of the induced navigation forces and quantify its error with respect to the fully actuated reference dynamics. An aggregated robust HOCBF-QP safety filter minimally modifies the nominal thrusts while guaranteeing pairwise collision avoidance with high probability.

</details>


### [287] [Joint Communication-Control Strategy Optimization with Partially Nested Information Structures: The Linear-Quadratic Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13535)
*Haoyi You,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了多智能体线性二次系统中联合通信与控制策略优化（JCCO）问题，在部分嵌套信息结构下，给出了保持部分嵌套性的条件，并开发了基于动态规划的求解方法，得到闭式Riccati方程，同时扩展到闭环通信策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中通信与控制策略的联合优化具有挑战性，现有方法在部分嵌套信息结构下的计算可处理性不足，且缺乏保持部分嵌套性的条件分析。

Method: 基于共同信息框架，形式化JCCO问题；针对开环通信策略，建立保持部分嵌套性的条件；开发动态规划方法推导最优控制策略，得到闭式Riccati方程；并扩展到闭环通信策略，提出更易处理的动态规划。

Result: 提出了保持部分嵌套性的条件，违反可能导致最优策略非线性；给出了开环通信策略下的闭式Riccati方程；闭环通信策略下得到比无限维CIB信念方法更易处理的动态规划。

Conclusion: 该工作为多智能体联合通信与控制优化提供了理论框架和计算方法，部分嵌套性条件有助于指导通信协议设计，动态规划方法具有独立价值，可推广到输出反馈的分散线性二次控制。

Abstract: In this paper, we formalize a joint communication-control strategy optimization (JCCO) problem in multi-agent linear systems with quadratic costs, under the common-information-based (CIB) framework from decentralized stochastic control. For computational tractability, we focus on such JCCO problems with partially nested (PN) information structures (ISs). In particular, with a baseline communication protocol that leads to a PN IS, we establish a series of conditions under which the partial nestedness is preserved under the (additional) communication strategies to be optimized, while violating them may cause nonlinearity of the optimal strategies in general, with open-loop communication strategies. We then develop a dynamic-programming-based approach to compute the optimal control strategies of JCCO with open-loop communication strategies, which yields a set of closed-form Riccati Equations. As a byproduct of independent interest, such an approach also offers a way to solve decentralized linear-quadratic control with PN ISs and output feedback, under the CIB framework. Finally, we extend such an approach to JCCOs with closed-loop communication strategies, yielding a more tractable dynamic program than an infinite-dimensional CIB-belief-based one.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [288] [SoM-MTM: Synesthesia of Machines (SoM)-Driven Masked Token Model for Cooperative Perception over Packet Loss Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13245)
*Haozhen Li,Rongqing Zhang,Xiang Cheng*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于机器联觉的掩码标记模型（SoM-MTM），作为通用视觉协同感知的即插即用范式，能够在丢包信道上恢复失真特征，提升感知性能并具备良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉协同感知研究受限于特定信道模型、合作模式和感知任务，缺乏通用性，无法满足大规模异构协同感知需求。

Method: 受掩码图像建模（如MAE）启发，提出SoM-MTM，基于Swin Transformer，通过外部路由混合专家机制嵌入先验掩码信息，修复和增强环境感知特征。

Result: 实验表明SoM-MTM在不同任务上持续提升感知性能，尤其在未见场景中表现强泛化，同时模型成本适中、可扩展性好。

Conclusion: SoM-MTM作为通用视觉协同感知范式，有效提高信息传递效率，增强协作感知能力，为通信与智能融合提供新思路。

Abstract: To support the large-scale and heterogeneous visual cooperative perception (CP) demands in next-generation mobile networks, intelligent and efficient sensory data transmission is a critical challenge. Under the emerging convergence of communication networks and agentic artificial intelligence (AI), existing research emphasizes utilizing end-to-end neural networks to simplify communication modules, which has shown promising potential for CP. However, these studies are still limited to specific channel models, cooperation modes, and perception tasks, failing to fully leverage powerful visual processing approaches to enhance universality. To address this, we propose a Synesthesia of Machines (SoM)-driven Masked Token Model, referred to as SoM-MTM, as a plug-and-play paradigm for generic visual CP. Inspired by masked image modeling methods such as MAE, it possesses great perceptual context learning capabilities to recover distorted features over packet loss channels, thereby improving information carrying efficiency. Building upon Swin Transformer, SoM-MTM further embeds prior masked information through an External Routing MoE mechanism, maximally repairing and enhancing environmental perception features during cooperation. Comprehensive experimental results confirm that SoM-MTM can consistently enhance perception performances on various tasks, especially strong generalization to unseen scenarios, while maintaining favorable model cost and scalability.

</details>
